\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 4. Branch Scope and Production Context\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Musical Theatre branch covers self-tape evaluation where acting and song are present, usually as a multi-component audition containing an acting scene and a song. It also covers MT-specific report behaviour around acting-through-song, vocal technique in service of story, scene-to-song integration, movement in service of story where visible, style/subtype language where observable, and fairness/access constraints.\
The completed revised MT outputs are intended to improve report specificity inside the existing TapeCoach architecture. They are not intended to change the scoring model, weights, score fields, audition types, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, report schema, backend pipeline or UI mechanisms.\
Current live production behaviour remains separate. The four supplied current MT outputs preserve the core Acting + Song structure, but they do not yet satisfy the v5 output-specificity standard. The revision and output-specificity patch are requirements for future implementation planning; they are not evidence that production has already changed.\
The branch must preserve the live system\'92s six score fields, current MT weights, acting + song detection, component breakdown, visible Acting and Vocal categories, consistency_modifier, timestamp coverage across acting and song, UK terminology, maximum 3 strengths, maximum 3 improvements and maximum 8 timestamped notes.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 5. Source and Provenance Summary\

\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Adversarial / non-regression tests: MT-V5-OST01\'96OST27 cover acting + song, no-brief MT, song-only and acting-only leakage, fixed/choice material, generic praise suppression, timestamp imbalance, false specificity, production polish, poor audio, access-adapted MT, callback/workshop overclaim, comparison mismatch and export timestamp mismatch.\
Display-layer checks: MT-V5-LQA01\'96LQA24 cover raw JSON, rendered report, PDF/export, comparison page, category/component labels, Vocal/Acting visibility, timestamp parity, score/verdict consistency, brief-mode consistency, role-fit suppression, reliability, presentation notes, technical signals and access-adapted output handling.\
Generic-feedback suppression: MT-V5-OSP01\'96OSP35 cover generic acting/vocal/movement/musicality phrases, recall/callback/castability overclaims, false-perfect-adherence language, alternative-repertoire leakage, polish/resource merit, access-deficit language and live-room inference.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 10. Live Output QA / Bridge QA Status\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 True current MT report artefacts were supplied as four PDF/report captures or excerpts. Live-output QA was completed at 
\f0\b partial PDF/report level
\f1\b0 . It was not full production release QA because raw JSON, comparison-page artefacts, separate rendered/export parity artefacts and score-recompute traces were not supplied.\
The four artefacts preserve the core MT anchor: Acting Scene and Song are visible, Vocal appears where singing exists, Acting appears where spoken acting exists, and component breakdown is displayed. However, current outputs fail MT-V5 output-specificity QA due to generic praise, unanchored acting-through-song, broad vocal praise, timestamp underproduction, brief/no-brief contradiction, readiness inconsistency and live-room/casting overclaims. Examples include \'93recall-worthy\'94 and only two timestamps in A01, the brief/no-brief contradiction and \'93WHY THIS ISN\'92T READY\'94 in A02, \'93strong callback potential\'94 and page-side claims in A03, and \'93highly castable for contemporary musical theatre workshops\'94 in A04.\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 14. Patch / Retest Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 Decision: No further branch research or rubric patch is required before cross-branch consolidation. The completed MT output-specificity patch and live-output QA failures must be carried into consolidation as P0 implementation-planning and retest requirements.
\f1\b0 \
Rationale:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls1\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 MT source research, synthesis, gap audit, revision, final audit and output-specificity mapping are complete.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 The branch should not be reopened for broad research or rubric rework.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Current live outputs fail the MT-V5 output-specificity standard, but that is an enforcement and QA problem, not a source-maturity problem.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Cross-branch consolidation can proceed if it carries forward the MT output-rule corrections and release blockers explicitly.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Production release sign-off must not proceed until corrected MT outputs pass live-output QA.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Patch scope, if treated operationally: output-rule enforcement, generic phrase suppression, timestamp density, brief/no-brief consistency, fixed-material boundaries, role-fit/callback suppression, presentation anti-polish handling, display/JSON/export parity QA.\
What must not be reopened: MT source research, MT weights, six score fields, subtype weights, backend/schema/data-flow, caps/blockers/verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, component detection architecture.\
Deep Research required: no.\
Standard ChatGPT sufficient: yes, for handoff, consolidation, QA mapping and implementation-planning requirements.\
Live outputs required for retest: yes. Required artefacts include raw JSON, rendered report, PDF/export, comparison page, score/verdict/cap trace and access-adapted MT output.\
Required retests: MT-V5-OST01\'96OST27 and MT-V5-LQA01\'96LQA24, prioritising no-brief MT, brief-mode contradiction, timestamp density, generic praise suppression, callback/castability suppression and JSON/render/export parity.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 15. Compact Branch Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Musical Theatre is complete at research, synthesis, audit, revision, final-audit and output-specificity mapping level. The strongest branch asset remains the stabilised MT anchor: musical_theatre detection where acting + song are present, acting and song component breakdown, visible Acting and Vocal categories, current MT weights, consistency_modifier where used, and timestamp coverage across acting and song. This must not regress.\
The evidence base is strong. Official admissions sources such as MT-B1-002, MT-B1-005, MT-B1-006, MT-B1-009, MT-B1-020, MT-B1-023, MT-B1-030 and [MT-S046] support audition task structure, acting + song evidence, training-potential language and the distinction between potential and perfect casting. Exam-board sources [MT-S004], [MT-S006], [MT-S007] and [MT-S009] support acting-through-song, vocal technique versus interpretation/communication, and performance/technique/reflection distinctions. Practitioner and fairness sources [MT-S055], [MT-S056], [MT-S057], [MT-S058], [MT-S064] and [MT-S065] support access, burden, authenticity and direction-response boundaries only where observable. Subtype/pedagogy sources [MT-S070], [MT-S072], [MT-S074], [MT-S077], [MT-S078] and [MT-S079] support style-aware report language but not fixed weights.\
MT-A01\'96MT-A15 were addressed in MT-REV and passed final audit. The revision preserved the six-field scoring model, current MT weights, Step 1 / Step 2 split, report schema, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds and role-fit bounds. It strengthened acting-through-song, lyric/phrase/beat evidence, vocal story/style distinctions, movement visibility, integration, self-tape assessability, anti-polish handling and anti-bias exclusions. Through-composed MT, fixed subtype weighting, deeper dance calibration and deeper vocal-style calibration remain deferred.\
The output-specificity package is complete through MT-V5-OSR01\'96OSR17, MT-V5-OSP patterns, MT-V5-OSA evidence anchors, MT-V5-OST tests and MT-V5-LQA requirements. However, current live MT outputs fail output-specificity QA. Four PDF/report artefacts preserve MT component structure but show generic praise, unanchored acting-through-song/vocal claims, timestamp underproduction, brief/no-brief contradiction, readiness inconsistency and callback/castability overclaims. Raw JSON, comparison-page parity, renderer/export timestamp parity and score recomputation traces are not verified.\
Decision: MT is ready for cross-branch consolidation as a completed research/revision branch with a mandatory output-enforcement and live-QA retest item. It is not ready for production release sign-off until corrected outputs pass MT-V5 live-output QA.\
Stable ID families used: MT-S###, MT-A##, MT-V5-I##, MT-V5-P##, MT-V5-OSR##, MT-V5-OSP##, MT-V5-OSA##, MT-V5-OST##, MT-V5-LQA##, MT-V5-LIVE-##.\
